{"type":"message_update","usage":{"input":764,"output":1557,"cacheRead":4096,"cacheWrite":0,"reasoning":0,"totalTokens":6417,"cost":{"input":0,"output":0,"cacheRead":0,"cacheWrite":0,"total":0}},"assistantMessageEvent":{"type":"thinking_end","contentIndex":0,"content":"\nSo `totals.py` has `column_totals` function that computes totals per column. But the export totals row in the test expects `\"TOTAL,4,6\"` for rows `[[1, 2], [3, 4]]`. That seems to be row totals? Actually, the rows are `[[1, 2], [3, 4]]`. The total of first column is 1+3=4, second column 2+4=6. So the totals row is `TOTAL,4,6`. So the export should append a row with \"TOTAL\" and the column totals.\n\n currently, `export_csv` calls `render_rows(rows)` which just renders each row as comma-separated values. To add a totals row, we need to compute column totals and then append a row like `[\"TOTAL\", total1, total2, ...]`.\n\n The `export_csv` function should probably be modified to accept an optional `totals` parameter. If `totals` is True, then after rendering the rows, we compute column totals and append a final row.\n\n Alternatively, `render_rows` could be modified to accept a `totals` parameter or to mark the last row as total. But the test `test_render_marks_last_row_as_total` shows `render_rows([[1, 2], [3, 4]], mark_last=True) == [\"1,2\", \"TOTAL,3,4\"]`. Wait, that test expects the last row to be marked as \"TOTAL,3,4\"? Let's see: input rows `[[1, 2], [3, 4]]`, if `mark_last=True`, it returns `[\"1,2\", \"TOTAL,3,4\"]`. That seems to mark the last row (which is `[3, 4]`) as \"TOTAL,3,4\". But the expected totals row in `test_export_appends_totals_row` is `\"TOTAL,4,6\"`, which is the total of all rows, not just the last row. So `render_rows` with `mark_last=True` is different from adding a separate totals row.\n\n Thus, the export functionality likely should use `column_totals` to compute the total per column and then append a row with \"TOTAL\" and the totals.\n\n Let's look at the test `test_export_appends_totals_row`: it expects `export_csv([[1, 2], [3, 4]], totals=True) == \"1,2\\n3,4\\nTOTAL,4,6\"`.
